## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的 AI 搭档。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 실제 작업을 대신 맡아 할 수 있어요. 예를 들어 깨진 테스트를 찾아서 고쳐 달라고 하면, 해당 저장소에서 작업하고 결과를 알려 드려요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요. 좋은 저녁 보내세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届いたデータを順番どおり、欠けなく確実に届ける」仕組み、UDPは「確実性より速さを優先し、届け漏れや順番の狂いを気にしない」仕組みです。

一言で言えば、**TCPは確実性、UDPは速さ**です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales que aún no has compartido, y merge para integrar cambios de una rama que otros ya usan. Nunca hagas rebase de commits que ya subiste y que otros pueden tener, porque reescribe la historia y les complica el trabajo.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 面向连接、保证可靠有序地送达；UDP 不建连接、不保证送达，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unpushed or private branch to keep history clean before you share it. Merge when others already have the branch, or when you want the real history of the work kept as it happened.
```

  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页：补端到端测试 | group: 登录页重做

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在后面就行：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，bun 会跑路径里包含这段字符的测试文件：

```bash
bun test foo
```

如果还想只跑文件里的某一个用例，加 `-t`：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts -t "某个用例的名字"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change to the data gets written down first, before it's applied to the database itself. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart to recover the changes that were committed but not yet saved to the main data files.
```

- 找到 PR review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把锁从粗的范围（整个调度器/整张表）缩到实际需要保护的最小范围（比如按 level 或按 segment 加锁）。

- 在同一分支上提交，PR 保持草稿状态。
- 完成后报告：改了哪几处锁、测试结果。

    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">–</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div>
  </div>

</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var LIMIT = 600;
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
    });
  }
  function render() {
    var trains = ducter.get('trains') || [];
    var limit = ducter.get('limit') || LIMIT;
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
    if (!trains.length) {
      list.innerHTML = '';
      empty.hidden = false;
      document.getElementById('count').textContent = '0 班';
      document.getElementById('sub').textContent = '';
      return;
    }
    empty.hidden = true;
    var sorted = trains.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.price - b.price; });
    var under = sorted.filter(function (t) { return t.price < limit; });
    document.getElementById('count').textContent = under.length + ' 班';
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = '共查到 ' + trains.length + ' 班，' + limit + ' 元以下的有 ' + under.length + ' 班';
    list.innerHTML = sorted.map(function (t) {
      var seats = t.seats != null ? '二等座余 ' + t.seats + ' 张' : '';
      var cls = t.price < limit ? 'k-row good' : 'k-row';
      return '<div class="' + cls + '"><div class="k-row-main">' + esc(t.name) +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">' + esc(seats) + '</div></div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value">¥' + esc(t.price) + '</div></div>';
    }).join('');
    ducter.summary(under.length + ' 班 600 元以下');
  }
  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"limit":600,"trains":[{"name":"G1","price":553,"seats":12},{"name":"G3","price":553,"seats":4},{"name":"G7","price":626,"seats":null}]}

(function () {
  function render(v) {
    var nights = Array.isArray(v) ? v.slice() : [];

      document.getElementById('latest-sub').textContent = '';
      empty.hidden = false;
      return;
    }
    empty.hidden = true;
    var top = nights[0];
    document.getElementById('latest').textContent = top.writes + ' writes/s';
    document.getElementById('latest-sub').textContent = 'Night ' + top.night + ' · p99 ' + top.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + top.stalls;
    nights.forEach(function (n) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');

      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = 'Night ' + n.night;
      var sub = document.createElement('div');
      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + n.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + n.stalls;
      main.appendChild(sub);
      var val = document.createElement('div');
      val.className = 'k-row-value';
      val.textContent = n.writes + ' writes/s';
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('nights')).then(render);
  ducter.onChange(render);
  ducter.summary('Night ' + '3' + ': 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms');
})();

    st.days.push(nextDay(last));
    st.cur = st.days[st.days.length - 1];
    save();
  };

  function load(v) {
    if (v && Array.isArray(v.days) && v.days.length) {
      st = { days: v.days, items: Array.isArray(v.items) ? v.items : [], cur: v.cur || v.days[0] };
    }
  }

  function refresh() {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(function (v) {
      load(v);
      render();
    });
  }

  ducter.onChange(refresh);
  resetForm();
  refresh();
})();

<section class="k-card">

  <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记下</button>
  <button class="k-btn" id="del" hidden>删掉这一笔</button>
</section>

<section class="k-card">

  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>今天还没有记录。</div>
</section>
</main>
<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const p = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
let editing = null;

function today() {
  const d = new Date();
  return d.getFullYear() + '-' + p(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + p(d.getDate());
}
function setNow() {
  const d = new Date();
  $('t').value = p(d.getHours()) + ':' + p(d.getMinutes());
}
function resetForm() {
  editing = null;
  $('formTitle').textContent = '记一笔';
  $('add').textContent  …     '</span><span class="k-row-value">' + f.ml + ' ml</span></button>'
  ).join('');
  $('empty').hidden = list.length > 0;
}

$('add').onclick = () => {
  const tm = $('t').value.trim();
  const ml = parseFloat($('ml').value);
  if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(tm) || !(ml > 0)) return;
  const [h, m] = tm.split(':');
  const t = today() + 'T' + p(+h) + ':' + m;
  let all = ducter.get('feeds') || [];
  if (editing) {
    all = all.map(f => (f.t === editing ? { t, ml } : f));
  } else {
    all.push({ t, ml });
  }
  ducter.set('feeds', all);
  resetForm();
  render();
};

$('del').onclick = () => {
  if (!editing) return;
  const all = (ducter.get('feeds') || []).filter(f => f.t !== editing);
  ducter.set('feeds', all);
  resetForm();
  render();
};

$('list').onclick = e => {
  const row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
  if (!row) return;
  const t = row.getAttribute('data-t');
  const f = (ducter.get('feeds') || []).find(x => x.t === t);
  if (!f) return;
  editing = t;
  $('t').value = f.t.slice(11, 16);
  $('ml').value = f.ml;
  $('formTitle').textContent = '改这一笔';
  $('add').textContent = '改好了';
  $('del').hidden = false;
};

ducter.onChange(render);
resetForm();
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"t":"2026-11-20T06:10","ml":120},{"t":"2026-11-20T09:05","ml":90}]}

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: "PRs waiting" is on your shelf with the four PRs, and ticking one off updates the count.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2144 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="n">4</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="s">of 4 PRs · 0 landed</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul>
</div>
<script>
var DEFAULT = [
  {id:"ducter-412", title:"wey-gu/ducter#412 · compaction cut", waiting:"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday", done:false},
  {id:"mem-10287", title:"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · retry on 502", waiting:"On CI, failing", done:false},
  {id:"hawdb-1592", title:"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · flaky compaction test", waiting:"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday", done:false},
  {id:"ducter-418", title:"wey-gu/ducter#418 · apps bar", waiting:"Draft", done:false}
];
function items() {
  var saved = ducter.get("prs");
  return Array.isArray(saved) ? saved : DEFAULT;
}
function render() {
  var list = items();
  var left = list.filter(function (p) { return !p.done; }).length;
  var landed = list.length - left;
  document.getElementById("n").textContent = left;
  document.getElementById("s").textContent = "of " + list.length + " PRs · " + landed + " landed";
  var ul = document.getElementById("list");
  ul.innerHTML = "";
  list.forEach(function (p, i) {
    var li = document.createElement("li");
    li.className = "k-row";
    li.innerHTML = '<label style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;width:100%;cursor:pointer">' +
      '<input type="checkbox" ' + (p.done ? "checked" : "") + ' style="margin-top:3px">' +
      '<span class="k-row-main" style="flex:1;' + (p.done ? "text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)" : "") + '">' +
      '<span>' + p.title + '</span>' +
      '<span class="k-row-sub">' + p.waiting + '</span>' +
      '</span></label>';
    li.querySelector("input").addEventListener("change", function (e) {
      var next = items().slice();
      next[i] = Object.assign({}, next[i], {done: e.target.checked});
      ducter.set("prs", next);
    });
    ul.appendChild(li);
  });
  ducter.summary(left + " waiting · " + landed + " landed");
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"wey-gu/ducter#412 · compaction cut","waiting":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday","done":false},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · retry on 502","waiting":"On CI, failing","done":false},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · flaky compaction test","waiting":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","done":false},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"wey-gu/ducter#418 · apps bar","waiting":"Draft","done":false}]}

function draw() {

    row.onclick = () => edit(i);
    const main = document.createElement("span");

    main.textContent = x.what || x.trip || "未命名";
    const sub = document.createElement("span");

    sub.textContent = (x.day || "") + "  " + (x.cat || "");
    const val = document.createElement("span");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.textContent = fmt(x.amt);
    row.append(main, sub, val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });

  ducter.summary(xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔,合计 " + fmt(total) : "暂无花销");

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一个奶油色的 3D 笔记本电脑插画：屏幕是空白的，边框是深藍色，背景透明。

适合放在空状态里。屏幕空白正好对应"还没有内容"，画风柔和，不会显得冷冰冰。用的时候注意几点：

- 尺寸控制在 120–160 px 左右，放在标题和说明文字上方，别撑满版面。
- 它颜色偏浅，放在白底上对比不够，可以试试浅灰或浅米色的卡片底。
- 深色模式下会显得很亮，最好换一个暗色版本，或者降低一点亮度。
- 配一句说明和一个按钮，比如"还没有笔记，新建一条试试"，这样用户知道下一步做什么。
```

### call-emergency-en

```
--- step 1: user says: My neighbour collapsed, call 911 for me now!
  [partner message] I can't call 911 for you. Please call them yourself right now, or ask someone nearby to. Tell them: "My neighbour has collapsed at [address]," and stay on the line.

While you wait:
- Check whether they're breathing normally. Tap their shoulder and shout, "Are you OK?"
- If they aren't responding or aren't breathing normally, start CPR: push hard and fast in the centre of the chest, about 100 to 120 compressions a minute. The dispatcher can talk you through it.
- If there's an AED nearby, turn it on and follow its voice prompts.
```
